> current state.

## 2026-06-21 Traceability Update - Integration Health Refresh UX

Evidence: `apps/api/src/threat-feeds/correlate.ts` and `tests/acceptance/threat-feed-alerts-flow.test.ts`.

Tests are in `apps/api/src/model-session-timeout.test.ts`, `apps/api/src/runner-task-result-state.test.ts`, `packages/connectors/src/siem-mock-observer-default.test.ts`, `packages/connectors/src/edr-waf-mock-observer-default.test.ts`, and `apps/api/src/runtime-services.test.ts`.
- **PRD-AIEndpointProbe-Truthfulness:** Implemented on branch `codex/ai-endpoint-truthfulness`. Live-safe AI endpoint probes now store probe results as `Inconclusive` even when the endpoint returns 2xx, keep the top-level run `Inconclusive`, and do not promote `endpoint_probe.benign_policy_boundary` signals into validated findings or Snapshot AI risk lists. Real Promptfoo/PyRIT/Garak harness signals remain eligible for AI findings. Files: `packages/modules/src/index.ts`, `apps/api/src/runtime-services.ts`, `docs/ACCEPTANCE_CRITERIA.md`, `docs/TRACEABILITY_MATRIX.md`. Tests: `packages/modules/src/index.test.ts`, `apps/api/src/runtime-services.test.ts`.

CI uses `actions/setup-go` with `apps/runner/go.mod` before `pnpm verify`, and `scripts/test-runner.sh` requires Go 1.22+ or falls back to `golang:1.22-alpine` for local parity. Files: `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `scripts/test-runner.sh`, `apps/runner/README.md`, `PRODUCTION_READINESS.md`. Tests: `pnpm test:runner`, `pnpm test:runner:lab`, `pnpm test:runner:deploy`.
- **PRD-OSS-DockerRuntimeHardening:** Implemented on branch `codex/oss-docker-hardening`. Docker-backed OSS module launches now use `buildHardenedDockerRunArgs` for read-only rootfs, tmpfs, non-root user, cap drop, no-new-privileges, pids/memory/CPU ceilings, minimal volumes, and explicit network mode. Files: `packages/modules/src/index.ts`, `docs/TOOL_RUNTIME_SECURITY.md`, `docs/OPEN_SOURCE_VALIDATION_ENGINES.md`. Tests: `packages/modules/src/index.test.ts`.
- **PRD-OSS-ManifestSafetyMetadata:** Implemented and contained in `codex/integrate-validated-pr-stack`; superseded PR #36 head `f84925f` is an ancestor of the active branch. `ModuleManifestSchema` exposes first-class runtime/safety metadata (`toolVersion`, `containerImage`, command metadata, `licenseRisk`, network posture, target write/modify/code/exfil flags, destructive potential, data sensitivity, redaction rules, local lab targets, maintainer, status). `scripts/module-certification.ts` fails unsafe metadata claims. Files: `packages/modules/src/index.ts`, `scripts/module-certification.ts`, `docs/OPEN_SOURCE_TOOL_ADAPTER_SPEC.md`, `docs/OPEN_SOURCE_VALIDATION_ENGINES.md`, `docs/TRACEABILITY_MATRIX.md`. Tests: `packages/modules/src/index.test.ts`, `tests/modules/module-certification.test.ts`.

The API-backed `/registries` surface renders module status, license risk, network posture, destructive potential, data sensitivity, target write/modify/code/exfil flags, runtime image, tool version, maintainer, and redaction rules from `/api/v1/modules`. ## 2026-06-19 Codex Slice — Enterprise OIDC Login

- **PRD-EnterpriseAccess-OIDCLogin**: Enterprise customers must be able to start and complete OIDC SSO login through public API routes, with tenant-scoped state/nonce verification, provisioned-user membership enforcement, session-cookie creation, audit events, password-login denial when SSO is enforced, and same-tenant SSO enforcement for cross-tenant switches.
  - Linked source: PRD enterprise/MSSP access requirements, API-first model, auditability requirements, tenant/RBAC safety boundaries.
  - Implementation files: `packages/shared/src/domain.ts`, `packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma`, `packages/db/prisma/migrations/20260619020000_add_tenant_sso_auth_requests/migration.sql`, `apps/api/src/app.ts`, `apps/api/src/openapi-payloads.ts`, `apps/api/src/runtime-services.ts`, `apps/api/src/services/sso.ts`, `apps/api/src/services/auth.ts`.
  - Tests: `packages/shared/src/domain.test.ts`, `packages/db/src/sso-schema.test.ts`, `apps/api/src/app.test.ts`, `tests/acceptance/tenant-sso-login-flow.test.ts`.
  - Status: Product-Done in repo (2026-06-19). Public `/api/v1/auth/sso/start` and GET/POST `/api/v1/auth/sso/callback` are API-driven; auth requests store only hashed state and nonce; callbacks exchange code at the configured token endpoint, verify ID token with JWKS/issuer/audience/nonce/email-domain constraints, create standard Periscan session cookies for active provisioned tenant members, reject replayed state, write `sso.login_*` audit events, block password login with `sso_required` when tenant SSO is enforced, reject password/legacy session tenant-switching into enforced-SSO tenants, and reject an SSO session issued for one tenant when it tries to enter a different enforced-SSO tenant.
  - Remaining external/customer work: configure an authorized IdP, client credentials, redirect URI, and any customer-specific claim conventions.
